# Construction assessment under the revised stopping condition

The user revised the stopping condition to require one meaningful positive partial proof for every practically eligible group. The current source-reviewed classification contains 53 eligible groups and 23 groups without a supported positive clause in the available setup. Every eligible group now has a named reusable proof and checked concrete application.

The 53 groups represent 69.7% of all 76 groups. The other 23 represent 30.3%; their reasons concern current source and evidence support, not mathematical impossibility. Several results support overlapping clauses in more than one group. The report does not claim 53 independent complete specifications.

See [practical-provability.md](practical-provability.md) for all 76 classifications, exact clause scopes, proof names and evidence paths. Final combined validation and coordinator review passed. See [the final validation record](validation/final-partial-coverage.md). Whole-document compliance remains unproved.

The original stronger assessment follows. Its unfinished work is retained for future work and is no longer a prerequisite for the revised stopping condition. Historical statements about exhaustive convergence below apply only to that stronger objective.

## Preserved stronger full-clause assessment

# Construction completion assessment

Updated 2026-10-24T20:23:38.016202+00:00. The run remains incomplete because actionable proof, importer and source-clause work remains. This document is a current work assessment, not a stopping justification.

All 76 encompassing specifications are present in the current source. The 749 indexed source passages include narrative context, recommendations and extraction fragments. They are accounting entries, not 749 independent requirements. Each group below separates the present specification, mapped general proofs, checked applications and remaining work.

A checked application means that a general theorem has been applied to concrete captured facts. A model execution is a sequence allowed by the formal processing rules. A positive execution is an explicit allowed sequence that reaches delivery. The source and implementation contracts identify how those rules represent Linux, FreeBSD and cryptographic behavior.

Declared implementation and collector contracts do not automatically make a proved clause unfinished. Unchecked conditions that are themselves source requirements remain gaps. Product approvals, authority decisions and organizational records remain separate from observed configuration facts. Retrieved annexes still require their technically checkable clauses to be decomposed and applied; an opaque annex-satisfied proposition does not discharge them.

Current packet work includes native IPv4/ESP, eight IPv4/IPv6 ESP combinations, exact early IPv6 header gates, distributed fragmentation, arbitrary inbound tunnel checks and mixed IPsec/MACsec/L2TP local-to-global proofs. The six repaired mixed configurations discharge their stack conditions. Eighteen checked positive mixed executions cover both directions and the separate bridged opaque-Ethernet case with zero, one and two protected VLAN tags. Seven applications prove actual Inner-before-Outer encryption events. Native per-byte applications now include copied prefixes, every component response case and actual Inner-before-Outer event ancestry. Mixed inbound no-bypass has separate local checks and positive paths. Authenticated encrypted reassembly, mixed per-byte composition and remaining backend connections remain active.

FreeBSD DNS v13 discharges loaded skip flags, complete rule/scrub/translation inventories and empty state preservation under a fixed configuration. Both guest applications compile and all 14 importer tests pass. Conventional DNS transport denial does not by itself discharge the remaining resolver/service and automatic-startup clauses.

Evidence-side all-group accounting comes from the immutable assessment-and-replay-v7 bundle. Later accepted response files are listed per group. Those observations are inputs to construction; their availability is not a formal completion claim.

Final convergence still requires current source-to-clause reconciliation, all accepted evidence applications, importer mutation tests, an integrated build, an axiom audit and representative-path validation. No remaining construction item in this document is accepted as intractable merely because it is unfinished.

- [TDN.Imported.FinalMACsecProtection.selected_protection_configuration](../../../../TDN/Evidence/FinalMACsecProtection.lean): All four final v18 controlled ports: enabled delay algorithm/callback, normal and bounded timers, zero replay window, full local encryption and must-secure; physical timing and lower-interface bypass scope remains explicit.
- [TDN.Imported.FinalMACsecProtection.runtime_inventory_nonempty](../../../../TDN/Evidence/FinalMACsecProtection.lean): Four actual final v18 ports across both EAP role arrangements.

Remaining work across the selected scope and other representative cases:

- Connect MD10 external-facing data traffic to every physical-parent transmit path, including direct lower-interface traffic. The controlled-port theorem covers its modeled transmit operation; it does not itself prohibit another path.
- Apply MD7/8/9/12/16 to other representative PSK/manual/XPN cases with their actual settings. The four final EAP ports are one selected configuration.
- Keep any claimed physical delay bound separate from enablement. Bind authenticated PN sample age, association continuity and receiver time to SequenceFreshness before asserting a universal age limit.

Current response inputs: [ASSESSMENT-REPLAY-v6](responses/ASSESSMENT-REPLAY-v6.json), [EAP-DELAY-v17](responses/EAP-DELAY-v17.json), [EAP-DELAY-v18](responses/EAP-DELAY-v18.json), [IEEE-KDF-REVIEW-v4](responses/IEEE-KDF-REVIEW-v4.json), [MKA-EVENTS-DELAY-v3](responses/MKA-EVENTS-DELAY-v3.json), [MKA-REPRODUCTION-v3](responses/MKA-REPRODUCTION-v3.json).

Evidence-side disposition in the immutable v7 assessment: finite-delay-repair-in-both-EAP-roles. Later response inputs may supersede that disposition; they remain construction tasks until imported and checked.

Source-passage accounting:

- CSFC-MSC-1.3.0-4e40e3d83708:p041:u012 — MSC-MD-7 MACsec Devices must enable data delay protection for MACsec Key Agreement (MKA). T=O
  Current passage record: partial_contribution_full_clause_unproved. Both final EAP arrangements bind the enabled timed-PN source, both receive callbacks, active delay-protect advertisements and four effective ports. Sixteen finite controls support the enabled mechanism; they do not establish an unconditional physical age theorem.
- CSFC-MSC-1.3.0-4e40e3d83708:p041:u013 — MSC-MD-8 MACsec Devices must have an MKA Lifetime Timeout limit set to 6.0 seconds and Hello Timeout limit set to 2.0 seconds. T=O
  Current passage record: partial_contribution_full_clause_unproved. Every final port reports normal Hello 2000 ms, active bounded Hello 500 ms and lifetime 6000 ms. INT-028 distinguishes the normal limit from the active bounded interval.
- CSFC-MSC-1.3.0-4e40e3d83708:p041:u014 — MSC-MD-9 MACsec Devices must have the replay window set to 2 or as low as possible given the nature of the Black Network being traversed. T=O
  Current passage record: partial_contribution_full_clause_unproved. Every final port has observed replay window zero and useful traffic. Zero is the least natural-number window; the argument does not assert that other values are infeasible.
- CSFC-MSC-1.3.0-4e40e3d83708:p041:u015 — MSC-MD-10 MACsec Devices must require all data traffic on an external facing port to be encrypted (e.g., must- secure). T=O
  Current passage record: partial_contribution_full_clause_unproved. Every modeled controlled-port data emission is fully encrypted, and absent associations block emission. Completeness of physical-parent data paths remains a separate MD10 obligation.
- CSFC-MSC-1.3.0-4e40e3d83708:p042:u003 — MSC-MD-12 MACsec Devices must have the Confidentiality Offset set to 0 (zero). T=O
  Current passage record: partial_contribution_full_clause_unproved. Final software-port settings and source binding establish confidentiality offset zero for the selected four ports.
- CSFC-MSC-1.3.0-4e40e3d83708:p042:u007 — MSC-MD-16 MACsec Devices must be configured to “must- secure”. T=O
  Current passage record: partial_contribution_full_clause_unproved. The exact loaded configuration uses macsec_policy=1, and the runtime controlled-port transmit contract requires protection.
